{"parentUuid":"c9ca738f-2649-4cac-b9de-36b536da9c80","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013E9vo2ksqishsz9h55FQrG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n2\t\n3\timport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\n4\timport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n5\timport {\n6\t  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n7\t  computeCreditPurchase,\n8\t} from \"./validation\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n12\t *\n13\t * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n14\t * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n15\t * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n16\t * existing key is the other path.\n17\t *\n18\t * REQUEST BODY:\n19\t * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n20\t * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n21\t * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n22\t *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n23\t *\n24\t * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n25\t * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n26\t * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n27\t *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n28\t *\n29\t * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n30\t * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n31\t * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n32\t * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n33\t * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n34\t */\n35\t\n36\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n37\t  try {\n38\t    const body = await req.json();\n39\t    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n40\t      licenseKey?: unknown;\n41\t      amount?: unknown;\n42\t      email?: unknown;\n43\t    };\n44\t\n45\t    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n46\t    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n47\t    if (amountError !== null) {\n48\t      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n49\t    }\n50\t\n51\t    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n52\t      amount as number\n53\t    );\n54\t\n55\t    const siteUrl =\n56\t      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n57\t\n58\t    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n59\t    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n60\t    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n61\t    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n62\t    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n63\t    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n64\t      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n65\t        ? licenseKey.trim()\n66\t        : undefined;\n67\t    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n68\t\n69\t    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n70\t    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n71\t    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n72\t    const guestEmail =\n73\t      !hasLicenseKey &&\n74\t      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n75\t      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n76\t        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n77\t        : undefined;\n78\t\n79\t    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n80\t      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n81\t      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n82\t      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n83\t    };\n84\t\n85\t    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n86\t    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n87\t    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n88\t\n89\t    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n90\t      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n91\t\n92\t      if (!license) {\n93\t        console.error(\n94\t          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n95\t          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n96\t        );\n97\t        return NextResponse.json(\n98\t          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n99\t          { status: 400 }\n100\t        );\n101\t      }\n102\t\n103\t      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n104\t      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n105\t      // them.\n106\t      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n107\t        return NextResponse.json(\n108\t          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n109\t          { status: 400 }\n110\t        );\n111\t      }\n112\t\n113\t      const email = license.email;\n114\t      if (!email) {\n115\t        return NextResponse.json(\n116\t          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n117\t          { status: 400 }\n118\t        );\n119\t      }\n120\t\n121\t      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n122\t\n123\t      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n124\t      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n125\t      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n126\t        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n127\t          email,\n128\t          limit: 1,\n129\t        });\n130\t\n131\t        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n132\t          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n133\t        } else {\n134\t          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n135\t            email,\n136\t            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n137\t          });\n138\t          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n139\t        }\n140\t\n141\t        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n142\t      }\n143\t    }\n144\t\n145\t    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n146\t    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n147\t    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n148\t      mode: \"payment\",\n149\t      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n150\t      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n151\t      ...(stripeCustomerId\n152\t        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n153\t        : {\n154\t            customer_creation: \"always\",\n155\t            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n156\t          }),\n157\t      line_items: [\n158\t        {\n159\t          price_data: {\n160\t            currency: \"usd\",\n161\t            product_data: {\n162\t              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n163\t              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n164\t              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n165\t              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n166\t              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n167\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n168\t            },\n169\t            unit_amount: creditCents,\n170\t          },\n171\t          quantity: 1,\n172\t        },\n173\t        {\n174\t          price_data: {\n175\t            currency: \"usd\",\n176\t            product_data: {\n177\t              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n178\t              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n179\t              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n180\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n181\t            },\n182\t            unit_amount: feeCents,\n183\t          },\n184\t          quantity: 1,\n185\t        },\n186\t      ],\n187\t\n188\t      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n189\t      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n190\t\n191\t      // Metadata for webhook processing\n192\t      metadata,\n193\t\n194\t      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n195\t      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n196\t\n197\t      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n198\t      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n199\t      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n200\t      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n201\t      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n202\t    });\n203\t\n204\t    // Return checkout URL to client\n205\t    if (session.url) {\n206\t      return NextResponse.json({\n207\t        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n208\t      });\n209\t    } else {\n210\t      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n211\t    }\n212\t  } catch (error) {\n213\t    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n214\t\n215\t    return NextResponse.json(\n216\t      {\n217\t        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n218\t        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n219\t      },\n220\t      { status: 500 }\n221\t    );\n222\t  }\n223\t}\n224\t"}]},"uuid":"b51c2ef4-120d-412f-8257-ff54db82fcce","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:26.648Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":224,"startLine":1,"totalLines":224}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c9ca738f-2649-4cac-b9de-36b536da9c80","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Used so balance reads can join the caller's transaction\n291\t// instead of opening a second connection (which sees a stale snapshot and ties\n292\t// up the pool while row locks are held).\n293\ttype DbExecutor = typeof db | Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];\n294\t\n295\t// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n296\t// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n297\t// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n298\t// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n299\t// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\n300\tconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n301\t\n302\t/**\n303\t * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n304\t * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n305\t * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n306\t * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n307\t * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n308\t */\n309\tasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n310\t  executor: DbExecutor,\n311\t  userId: string\n312\t): Promise<number> {\n313\t  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n314\t    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n315\t    FROM credit_grants\n316\t    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n317\t      AND status = 'active'\n318\t      AND remaining_amount > 0\n319\t      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n320\t  `);\n321\t  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n322\t}\n323\t\n324\t/**\n325\t * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n326\t * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n327\t * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n328\t * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n329\t * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n330\t */\n331\tasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n332\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n333\t  userId: string\n334\t): Promise<number> {\n335\t  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n336\t  await tx\n337\t    .insert(creditBalances)\n338\t    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n339\t    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n340\t      target: creditBalances.userId,\n341\t      set: {\n342\t        balance: total.toString(),\n343\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n344\t      },\n345\t    });\n346\t  return total;\n347\t}\n348\t\n349\tasync function incrementCreditBalance(\n350\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n351\t  userId: string,\n352\t  amount: number\n353\t): Promise<number> {\n354\t  const [row] = await tx\n355\t    .insert(creditBalances)\n356\t    .values({ userId, balance: amount.toString() })\n357\t    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n358\t      target: creditBalances.userId,\n359\t      set: {\n360\t        balance: sql`${creditBalances.balance} + ${amount}`,\n361\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n362\t      },\n363\t    })\n364\t    .returning({ balance: creditBalances.balance });\n365\t\n366\t  return Number(row.balance);\n367\t}\n368\t\n369\t// Every credit grant expires one year after it is created (OpenRouter model):\n370\t// the ToS reserves the right to expire unused credits 365 days after purchase.\n371\t// Stamped here so it covers every insert path (paid packs, minted keys, admin\n372\t// grants); enforcement is the lazy `expires_at > now()` filter on reads/spends.\n373\tconst CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n374\t\n375\tasync function grantCreditLotInTransaction(\n376\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n377\t  data: CreditGrantInsert\n378\t): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n379\t  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS);\n380\t  const [grantRow] = await tx\n381\t    .insert(creditGrants)\n382\t    .values({\n383\t      userId: data.userId,\n384\t      sourceType: data.sourceType,\n385\t      sourceId: data.sourceId,\n386\t      originalAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n387\t      remainingAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n388\t      refundedAmount: \"0\",\n389\t      status: \"active\",\n390\t      expiresAt,\n391\t    })\n392\t    .onConflictDoNothing({\n393\t      target: [creditGrants.sourceType, creditGrants.sourceId],\n394\t    })\n395\t    .returning({ id: creditGrants.id });\n396\t\n397\t  if (!grantRow) {\n398\t    // Read inside the transaction so the balance reflects the locked, in-tx\n399\t    // view rather than a stale snapshot from a second pooled connection.\n400\t    const balance = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, data.userId);\n401\t    return { status: \"duplicate\", balance };\n402\t  }\n403\t\n404\t  const balance = await incrementCreditBalance(tx, data.userId, data.amount);\n405\t  return { status: \"processed\", balance };\n406\t}\n407\t\n408\texport async function grantCreditLot(\n409\t  data: CreditGrantInsert\n410\t): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n411\t  return db.transaction((tx) => grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, data));\n412\t}\n413\t\n414\texport async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n415\t  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n416\t): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n417\t  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n418\t    const [eventRow] = await tx\n419\t      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)"}]},"uuid":"5be39ec8-5121-41f2-97f3-e5b8b528ead0","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:34.462Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"// transaction handle. Used so balance reads can join the caller's transaction\n// instead of opening a second connection (which sees a stale snapshot and ties\n// up the pool while row locks are held).\ntype DbExecutor = typeof db | Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];\n\n// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\nconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n\n/**\n * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n */\nasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n  executor: DbExecutor,\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n    FROM credit_grants\n    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n      AND status = 'active'\n      AND remaining_amount > 0\n      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n  `);\n  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n}\n\n/**\n * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n */\nasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n  await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: total.toString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    });\n  return total;\n}\n\nasync function incrementCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<number> {\n  const [row] = await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: amount.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: sql`${creditBalances.balance} + ${amount}`,\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    })\n    .returning({ balance: creditBalances.balance });\n\n  return Number(row.balance);\n}\n\n// Every credit grant expires one year after it is created (OpenRouter model):\n// the ToS reserves the right to expire unused credits 365 days after purchase.\n// Stamped here so it covers every insert path (paid packs, minted keys, admin\n// grants); enforcement is the lazy `expires_at > now()` filter on reads/spends.\nconst CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\nasync function grantCreditLotInTransaction(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS);\n  const [grantRow] = await tx\n    .insert(creditGrants)\n    .values({\n      userId: data.userId,\n      sourceType: data.sourceType,\n      sourceId: data.sourceId,\n      originalAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      remainingAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      refundedAmount: \"0\",\n      status: \"active\",\n      expiresAt,\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({\n      target: [creditGrants.sourceType, creditGrants.sourceId],\n    })\n    .returning({ id: creditGrants.id });\n\n  if (!grantRow) {\n    // Read inside the transaction so the balance reflects the locked, in-tx\n    // view rather than a stale snapshot from a second pooled connection.\n    const balance = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, data.userId);\n    return { status: \"duplicate\", balance };\n  }\n\n  const balance = await incrementCreditBalance(tx, data.userId, data.amount);\n  return { status: \"processed\", balance };\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditLot(\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  return db.transaction((tx) => grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, data));\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const [eventRow] = await tx\n      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)","numLines":130,"startLine":290,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b1af0cca-459b-4ef7-8937-ca8137acaaf8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"326bf240-6882-4767-bdde-d1ce40a81dac","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JYr2xvicagMRQeksYYtMDw","type":"tool_result","content":"555\texport async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n556\t  userId: string,\n557\t  amount: number\n558\t): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n559\t  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n560\t    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n561\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n562\t      FROM credit_grants\n563\t      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n564\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n565\t        AND status = 'active'\n566\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n567\t      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n568\t      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n569\t      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n570\t      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n571\t      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n572\t      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n573\t      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n574\t      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n575\t      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n576\t      ORDER BY\n577\t        expires_at ASC,\n578\t        created_at ASC,\n579\t        id\n580\t      FOR UPDATE\n581\t    `);\n582\t\n583\t    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n584\t    let deductedAmount = 0;\n585\t\n586\t    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n587\t      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n588\t\n589\t      const grant = result.rows[index];\n590\t      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n591\t      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n592\t\n593\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n594\t\n595\t      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n596\t      await tx\n597\t        .update(creditGrants)\n598\t        .set({\n599\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n600\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n601\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n602\t        })\n603\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n604\t\n605\t      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n606\t      deductedAmount += deduction;\n607\t    }\n608\t\n609\t    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n610\t    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n611\t    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n612\t    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n613\t    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n614\t    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n615\t\n616\t    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n617\t  });\n618\t}\n619\t\n620\t/**\n621\t * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n622\t *\n623\t * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. The public API response shape is\n624\t * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n625\t */\n626\texport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n627\t  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n628\t  return result.balance;\n629\t}\n630\t\n631\t/**\n632\t * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n633\t *\n634\t * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n635\t * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n636\t * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n637\t * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n638\t * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n639\t */\n640\texport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n641\t  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n642\t    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n643\t      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n644\t    }),"}]},"uuid":"e6706882-4b44-4726-91a3-2c5f23220319","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:35.230Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"export async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n      ORDER BY\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n    let deductedAmount = 0;\n\n    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n\n      const grant = result.rows[index];\n      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n\n      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n      deductedAmount += deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n\n    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n *\n * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. The public API response shape is\n * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n */\nexport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n  return result.balance;\n}\n\n/**\n * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n *\n * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n */\nexport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n    }),","numLines":90,"startLine":555,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"326bf240-6882-4767-bdde-d1ce40a81dac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"079ccb9c-8b2f-4a9a-a117-274ea0d57e6c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AGSMsyhfQiCgX2e6KEuier","type":"tool_result","content":"460\t    }>(sql`\n461\t      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n462\t      FROM credit_grants\n463\t      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n464\t        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n465\t      FOR UPDATE\n466\t    `);\n467\t\n468\t    const grant = target.rows[0];\n469\t    if (!grant) {\n470\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n471\t    }\n472\t\n473\t    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n474\t    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n475\t    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n476\t    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n477\t    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n478\t    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n479\t    if (clawback <= 0) {\n480\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n481\t    }\n482\t\n483\t    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n484\t    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n485\t    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n486\t    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n487\t    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n488\t    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n489\t    let toClawback = Math.min(\n490\t      clawback,\n491\t      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n492\t    );\n493\t\n494\t    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n495\t    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n496\t    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n497\t    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n498\t    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n499\t    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n500\t    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n501\t    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n502\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n503\t      FROM credit_grants\n504\t      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n505\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n506\t        AND status = 'active'\n507\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n508\t      ORDER BY\n509\t        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n510\t        expires_at ASC,\n511\t        created_at ASC,\n512\t        id\n513\t      FOR UPDATE\n514\t    `);\n515\t\n516\t    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n517\t      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n518\t      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n519\t      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n520\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n521\t      const newRemaining = rowRemaining - deduction;\n522\t      await tx\n523\t        .update(creditGrants)\n524\t        .set({\n525\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n526\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n527\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n528\t        })\n529\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, row.id));\n530\t      toClawback -= deduction;\n531\t    }\n532\t\n533\t    // Mark the refunded grant as refunded and record the full reclaimed value on\n534\t    // it (even when the credits were physically drawn from other grants), so the\n535\t    // grant's lifetime is correct and a duplicate refund webhook is a no-op.\n536\t    await tx\n537\t      .update(creditGrants)\n538\t      .set({\n539\t        remainingAmount: \"0\",\n540\t        refundedAmount: (alreadyRefunded + clawback).toString(),\n541\t        status: \"refunded\",\n542\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n543\t      })\n544\t      .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n545\t\n546\t    // Reconcile the cache to the authoritative grant total, healing any drift.\n547\t    await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, grant.user_id);\n548\t    return {\n549\t      status: \"processed\",\n550\t      refundedAmount: clawback,\n551\t    };\n552\t  });\n553\t}\n554\t"}]},"uuid":"f56b5398-2822-4483-9de5-20f55368c874","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:08.290Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"    }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    const grant = target.rows[0];\n    if (!grant) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n    if (clawback <= 0) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n    let toClawback = Math.min(\n      clawback,\n      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n    );\n\n    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      ORDER BY\n        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n      const newRemaining = rowRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, row.id));\n      toClawback -= deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Mark the refunded grant as refunded and record the full reclaimed value on\n    // it (even when the credits were physically drawn from other grants), so the\n    // grant's lifetime is correct and a duplicate refund webhook is a no-op.\n    await tx\n      .update(creditGrants)\n      .set({\n        remainingAmount: \"0\",\n        refundedAmount: (alreadyRefunded + clawback).toString(),\n        status: \"refunded\",\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      })\n      .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n    // Reconcile the cache to the authoritative grant total, healing any drift.\n    await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, grant.user_id);\n    return {\n      status: \"processed\",\n      refundedAmount: clawback,\n    };\n  });\n}\n","numLines":95,"startLine":460,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"079ccb9c-8b2f-4a9a-a117-274ea0d57e6c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4629244d-23b6-4eeb-a727-b43ba2f8f386","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"7e3ac550-789f-4a9e-9ffa-0f4c425e6415","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"4bffe15c-de8d-418d-8616-2be4ebef69e2","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018rdZk1nW1mboVy3qcNerPT","type":"tool_result","content":"BillingCard.tsx\nCloudCreditsCard.tsx\nCreditHistoryCard.tsx\nindex.ts\nLicenseKeysCard.tsx\n/**\n * Admin Customers Router\n *\n * Customer management for the admin dashboard.\n * All procedures require admin authentication.\n */\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { TRPCError } from \"@trpc/server\";\nimport Stripe from \"stripe\";\n\nimport { createTRPCRouter, adminProcedure } from \"../../trpc\";\nimport {\n  getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin,\n  searchAccountKeysByEmail,\n  getOrCreateUser,\n  insertAccountKey,\n  findAccountByKey,\n  findAccountById,\n  getCreditBalance,\n  grantCreditLot,\n  refundCreditGrant,\n  updateAccountKey,\n  getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds,\n  getUserById,\n  getUserByEmail,\n  getUsersByIds,\n  updateCustomerEmail,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { generateLicenseKey } from \"@/lib/services/license-key\";\nimport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\n\nconst stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {\n  apiVersion: \"2025-02-24.acacia\" as any,\n});\n\nconst MAX_ADMIN_CREDIT_GRANT = 1_000_000;\n\nexport const customersRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n  /**\n   * List customers, one row per customer (grouped by user), each carrying all\n   * of that customer's license keys. Supports optional email search filter.\n   *\n   * A search matches individual license rows, but we then expand to each\n   * matched customer's FULL license set (via their userId) so the license\n   * count, total credits, and \"moves all N licenses\" copy reflect the true\n   * totals — not just the licenses whose email matched the search term.\n   */\n  list: adminProcedure\n    .input(z.object({ search: z.string().optional() }).optional())\n    .query(async ({ input }) => {\n      try {\n        const search = input?.search?.trim();\n        const matched = search\n          ? await searchAccountKeysByEmail(search)\n          : await getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(1000);\n\n        // Expand search matches to each customer's full license set so counts\n        // and credits are true totals. The no-search path already has every\n        // license. Then pull canonical user.email for display.\n        const userIds = Array.from(new Set(matched.map((l) => l.userId)));","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"3a8a9ab7-ffd3-46e1-bac1-f16fc8f54b21","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:48:16.247Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"BillingCard.tsx\nCloudCreditsCard.tsx\nCreditHistoryCard.tsx\nindex.ts\nLicenseKeysCard.tsx\n/**\n * Admin Customers Router\n *\n * Customer management for the admin dashboard.\n * All procedures require admin authentication.\n */\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { TRPCError } from \"@trpc/server\";\nimport Stripe from \"stripe\";\n\nimport { createTRPCRouter, adminProcedure } from \"../../trpc\";\nimport {\n  getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin,\n  searchAccountKeysByEmail,\n  getOrCreateUser,\n  insertAccountKey,\n  findAccountByKey,\n  findAccountById,\n  getCreditBalance,\n  grantCreditLot,\n  refundCreditGrant,\n  updateAccountKey,\n  getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds,\n  getUserById,\n  getUserByEmail,\n  getUsersByIds,\n  updateCustomerEmail,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { generateLicenseKey } from \"@/lib/services/license-key\";\nimport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\n\nconst stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {\n  apiVersion: \"2025-02-24.acacia\" as any,\n});\n\nconst MAX_ADMIN_CREDIT_GRANT = 1_000_000;\n\nexport const customersRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n  /**\n   * List customers, one row per customer (grouped by user), each carrying all\n   * of that customer's license keys. Supports optional email search filter.\n   *\n   * A search matches individual license rows, but we then expand to each\n   * matched customer's FULL license set (via their userId) so the license\n   * count, total credits, and \"moves all N licenses\" copy reflect the true\n   * totals — not just the licenses whose email matched the search term.\n   */\n  list: adminProcedure\n    .input(z.object({ search: z.string().optional() }).optional())\n    .query(async ({ input }) => {\n      try {\n        const search = input?.search?.trim();\n        const matched = search\n          ? await searchAccountKeysByEmail(search)\n          : await getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(1000);\n\n        // Expand search matches to each customer's full license set so counts\n        // and credits are true totals. The no-search path already has every\n        // license. {"parentUuid":"da7c20a3-63f4-4f2c-920b-668ecf3ef191","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"4bffe15c-de8d-418d-8616-2be4ebef69e2","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QdiJgie9DttoGFqMG7cxCY","type":"tool_result","content":"  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(`Failed to resolve minted license for session ${session.id}`);\n  }\n\n  // STEP 2: Grant the purchased credits (no included bundle on credit purchases).\n  // Idempotent on session.id, so safe to run on every delivery.\n  const grantResult = await grantCreditsForStripeEvent({\n    eventId,\n    eventType,\n    stripeObjectId: session.id,\n    userId: license.userId,\n    creditAmount,\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: session.id,\n  });\n\n  // Retry/duplicate delivery: credits already granted for this session — don't\n  // re-send the email. (Pooling resolves the same license by email each retry,\n  // so this guard is what keeps a re-delivery from emailing twice.)\n  if (grantResult === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit purchase already processed for session ${session.id}, skipping email`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Email. When we pooled into an existing key, send a top-up receipt\n  // with the new balance (the buyer already has this key). A genuinely new key\n  // gets the mint email with its starting balance.\n  if (pooledIntoExisting) {\n    console.log(\n      `Pooled ${creditAmount} credits into existing key ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... for ${customerEmail}`\n    );\n\n    const newBalance = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n    const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditTopUp({\n      customerName,\n      customerEmail,\n      licenseKey: license.key,\n      creditAmount,\n      newBalance,\n      productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n      supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n    });\n\n    if (!emailResult.success) {\n      console.error(`Failed to send top-up email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `Minted license ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... with ${creditAmount} credits for ${customerEmail}`\n  );\n\n  const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditMint({\n    customerName,\n    customerEmail,\n    licenseKey: license.key,\n    creditAmount,\n    productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n    supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n  });\n\n  if (!emailResult.success) {\n    console.error(`Failed to send mint email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Process a charge refund.\n *\n * - \"license\" purchases: revoke the associated license.\n * - \"credits\" purchases: deduct the granted credits from the balance.\n *\n * REFUND SCOPE (per purchase type):\n * - license: acts only on a FULL refund (amount_refunded === amount).\n * - credits: the 6% processing fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a\n *   policy-compliant credit refund refunds only the credit value — a *partial*\n *   Stripe refund. Acts once amount_refunded covers the credit portion\n *   (charge total minus the non-refundable fee).\n *\n * TRACE PATH:\n * Charge -> PaymentIntent -> Checkout Session -> license_keys.stripe_session_id\n *\n * IDEMPOTENCY:\n * License revocation is idempotent (\"revoked\" status). {"parentUuid":"e7f6947a-7e66-4358-9cea-373adc747fde","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3877233-d111-4961-bc3e-0b2f869ea728","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01H63yovLtusMhC8Se93Vwzz","type":"tool_result","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { api } from \"@/lib/trpc/client\";\n\n/**\n * Credit History Card\n *\n * Lists the user's paid credit-pack purchases (top-ups and mints), newest-first,\n * with the amount, purchase date, expiry, and remaining balance per grant.\n * Paid packs only — free/included bundles are not shown here.\n */\nexport default function CreditHistoryCard() {\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = api.customer.creditHistory.useQuery();\n\n  // Hide the card entirely when there's nothing to show (no purchases yet).\n  if (isLoading || error) return null;\n  if (!data || data.grants.length === 0) return null;\n\n  const formatDate = (iso: string) =>\n    new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\n      year: \"numeric\",\n      month: \"short\",\n      day: \"numeric\",\n    });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 mb-4\">Credit Purchases</p>\n\n      <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n        {data.grants.map((grant) => (\n          <div\n            key={grant.id}\n            className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-white/5 pb-3 last:border-0 last:pb-0\"\n          >\n            <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n              <p className=\"text-white font-medium\">\n                {grant.originalAmount.toLocaleString()} credits\n              </p>\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n                {formatDate(grant.createdAt)}\n                {grant.expiresAt && (\n                  <>\n                    {\" · \"}\n                    {grant.expired ? (\n                      <span className=\"text-red-400\">\n                        expired {formatDate(grant.expiresAt)}\n                      </span>\n                    ) : (\n                      <span>expires {formatDate(grant.expiresAt)}</span>\n                    )}\n                  </>\n                )}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"text-right shrink-0\">\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-300\">\n                {grant.expired ? 0 : grant.remainingAmount.toLocaleString()} left\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7ea5c9b9-ea3f-4d6b-889c-5d6ff45f0d84","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:14.422Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { api } from \"@/lib/trpc/client\";\n\n/**\n * Credit History Card\n *\n * Lists the user's paid credit-pack purchases (top-ups and mints), newest-first,\n * with the amount, purchase date, expiry, and remaining balance per grant.\n * Paid packs only — free/included bundles are not shown here.\n */\nexport default function CreditHistoryCard() {\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = api.customer.creditHistory.useQuery();\n\n  // Hide the card entirely when there's nothing to show (no purchases yet).\n  if (isLoading || error) return null;\n  if (!data || data.grants.length === 0) return null;\n\n  const formatDate = (iso: string) =>\n    new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\n      year: \"numeric\",\n      month: \"short\",\n      day: \"numeric\",\n    });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 mb-4\">Credit Purchases</p>\n\n      <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n        {data.grants.map((grant) => (\n          <div\n            key={grant.id}\n            className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-white/5 pb-3 last:border-0 last:pb-0\"\n          >\n            <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n              <p className=\"text-white font-medium\">\n                {grant.originalAmount.toLocaleString()} credits\n              </p>\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n                {formatDate(grant.createdAt)}\n                {grant.expiresAt && (\n                  <>\n                    {\" · \"}\n                    {grant.expired ? (\n                      <span className=\"text-red-400\">\n                        expired {formatDate(grant.expiresAt)}\n                      </span>\n                    ) : (\n                      <span>expires {formatDate(grant.expiresAt)}</span>\n                    )}\n                  </>\n                )}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"text-right shrink-0\">\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-300\">\n                {grant.expired ? 0 : grant.remainingAmount.toLocaleString()} left\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e7f6947a-7e66-4358-9cea-373adc747fde","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"2caf7c6e-a053-4d4e-924d-779c6c6ac6eb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3877233-d111-4961-bc3e-0b2f869ea728","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UXSaYSeaEmdMDoHw1YhrgH","type":"tool_result","content":"        const userIds = Array.from(new Set(matched.map((l) => l.userId)));\n        const licenses = search\n          ? await getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds(userIds)\n          : matched;\n        const userMap = await getUsersByIds(userIds);\n\n        // Group licenses by their owning user. The source rows are ordered\n        // newest-first, so Map insertion order keeps customers in that order.\n        const customerMap = new Map<\n          string,\n          {\n            userId: string;\n            email: string;\n            licenseCount: number;\n            totalCredits: number;\n            created: number;\n            licenses: Array<{\n              id: string;\n              key: string;\n              status: string;\n              credits: number;\n              stripeSessionId: string | null;\n              stripeCustomerId: string | null;\n              created: number;\n            }>;\n          }\n        >();\n\n        for (const l of licenses) {\n          const created = Math.floor(l.createdAt.getTime() / 1000);\n          let customer = customerMap.get(l.userId);\n          if (!customer) {\n            customer = {\n              userId: l.userId,\n              // Canonical login email from the user table; fall back to the\n              // license email only if the user row is somehow missing.\n              email: (userMap.get(l.userId)?.email ?? l.email).toLowerCase(),\n              licenseCount: 0,\n              totalCredits: 0,\n              created,\n              licenses: [],\n            };\n            customerMap.set(l.userId, customer);\n          }\n          customer.licenseCount += 1;\n          // Credits are pooled per account, so every one of this customer's\n          // licenses reports the SAME account balance. Set the total once rather\n          // than summing per license (which would multiply it by the key count).\n          customer.totalCredits = l.credits;\n          // Show the customer's earliest license date as their \"Created\".\n          if (created < customer.created) customer.created = created;\n          customer.licenses.push({\n            id: l.id,\n            key: l.key,\n            status: l.status,\n            credits: l.credits,\n            stripeSessionId: l.stripeSessionId,\n            stripeCustomerId: l.stripeCustomerId,\n            created,\n          });\n        }\n\n        return { customers: Array.from(customerMap.values()) };","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"aa8b564f-fb28-479f-b060-e8fd2a595770","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:14.625Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"        const userIds = Array.from(new Set(matched.map((l) => l.userId)));\n        const licenses = search\n          ? await getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds(userIds)\n          : matched;\n        const userMap = await getUsersByIds(userIds);\n\n        // Group licenses by their owning user. {"parentUuid":"19cc72ee-360a-4697-b135-d0446aa303e1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2445ddd5-e2fb-4a7c-85fc-daeba592d22c","agentId":"a5d6827d8ff2888aa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011fzkpHQ1dV21kvAxWuzrTo","type":"tool_result","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { LicenseKeysCard, CloudCreditsCard, CreditHistoryCard, BillingCard } from \"@/components/customer/dashboard\";\nimport { api } from \"@/lib/trpc/client\";\n\ninterface UserDashboardClientProps {\n  user: {\n    email: string;\n    id: string;\n  };\n  isAdmin: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * Unified User Dashboard Client\n *\n * Displays customer features (licenses, credits, billing).\n */\nexport default function UserDashboardClient({\n  user,\n  isAdmin,\n}: UserDashboardClientProps) {\n  // Always fetch customer data (licenses, credits)\n  const {\n    data,\n    isLoading,\n    error,\n  } = api.customer.licensesWithCredits.useQuery();\n\n  // Credits are pooled per account, so use the query's account-level totals\n  // rather than summing per license (which double-counts a multi-key account's\n  // pooled balance).\n  const totalCredits = data?.totalCredits ?? 0;\n  const totalMinutesRemaining = data?.totalMinutesRemaining ?? 0;\n  const activeLicenseKey = data?.licenses?.find((l) => l.status === \"granted\")?.key ?? null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div>\n        <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-white\">Dashboard</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1\">{user.email}</p>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* License Keys Card */}\n      {isLoading ? (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <div className=\"w-4 h-4 border-2 border-emerald-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n            <span className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm\">Loading Account Keys...</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ) : error ? (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n          <p className=\"text-red-400 text-sm\">{error.message}</p>\n        </div>\n      ) : data?.licenses && data.licenses.length > 0 ? (\n        <>\n          <LicenseKeysCard licenses={data.licenses} />\n          <CloudCreditsCard\n            totalCredits={totalCredits}\n            totalMinutesRemaining={totalMinutesRemaining}\n            creditsPerMinute={data.creditsPerMinute}\n            activeLicenseKey={activeLicenseKey}\n          />\n          <CreditHistoryCard />\n        </>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-gray-400\">No Account Keys found</p>\n          <a href=\"/\" className=\"text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 text-sm mt-2 inline-block\">\n            Get an Account Key →\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Billing */}\n      <BillingCard />\n\n      {/* Help */}\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">\n        Need help?{\" \"}\n        <a href=\"mailto:support@hyperwhisper.com\" className=\"text-gray-400 hover:text-white\">\n          support@hyperwhisper.com\n        </a>\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n=== BillingCard\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { api } from \"@/lib/trpc/client\";\n\nexport default function BillingCard() {\n  const { data: billing } = api.customer.billingProviders.useQuery();\n  const stripePortalMutation = api.customer.stripePortalUrl.useMutation({\n    onSuccess: (data) => window.open(data.url, \"_blank\"),\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3\">Billing & Invoices</p>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-3\">\n        {billing?.hasStripe && (\n          <button\n            onClick={() => stripePortalMutation.mutate()}\n            disabled={stripePortalMutation.isPending}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-300 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-lg transition-colors disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {stripePortalMutation.isPending ? 0;\n  const totalMinutesRemaining = data?.totalMinutesRemaining ?? 0;\n  const activeLicenseKey = data?.licenses?.find((l) => l.status === \"granted\")?.key ?? null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div>\n        <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-white\">Dashboard</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1\">{user.email}</p>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* License Keys Card */}\n      {isLoading ? (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <div className=\"w-4 h-4 border-2 border-emerald-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n            <span className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm\">Loading Account Keys...</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ) : error ? (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n          <p className=\"text-red-400 text-sm\">{error.message}</p>\n        </div>\n      ) : data?.licenses && data.licenses.length > 0 ? (\n        <>\n          <LicenseKeysCard licenses={data.licenses} />\n          <CloudCreditsCard\n            totalCredits={totalCredits}\n            totalMinutesRemaining={totalMinutesRemaining}\n            creditsPerMinute={data.creditsPerMinute}\n            activeLicenseKey={activeLicenseKey}\n          />\n          <CreditHistoryCard />\n        </>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-gray-400\">No Account Keys found</p>\n          <a href=\"/\" className=\"text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 text-sm mt-2 inline-block\">\n            Get an Account Key →\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Billing */}\n      <BillingCard />\n\n      {/* Help */}\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">\n        Need help?{\" \"}\n        <a href=\"mailto:support@hyperwhisper.com\" className=\"text-gray-400 hover:text-white\">\n          support@hyperwhisper.com\n        </a>\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n=== BillingCard\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { api } from \"@/lib/trpc/client\";\n\nexport default function BillingCard() {\n  const { data: billing } = api.customer.billingProviders.useQuery();\n  const stripePortalMutation = api.customer.stripePortalUrl.useMutation({\n    onSuccess: (data) => window.open(data.url, \"_blank\"),\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 p-5\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3\">Billing & Invoices</p>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-3\">\n        {billing?.hasStripe && (\n          <button\n            onClick={() => stripePortalMutation.mutate()}\n            disabled={stripePortalMutation.isPending}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-300 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-lg transition-colors disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {stripePortalMutation.isPending ? \"Opening...\" : \"Stripe Portal\"}\n          </button>\n        )}\n        {billing?.hasPolar && (\n          <a\n            href=\"https://polar.sh/hyperwhisper/portal\"\n            target=\"_blank\"\n            rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-300 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-lg transition-colors\"\n          >\n            Polar Portal\n          </a>\n        )}\n      </div>\n      {stripePortalMutation.error && (\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-red-400 mt-2\">\n          {stripePortalMutation.error.message}\n        </p>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19cc72ee-360a-4697-b135-d0446aa303e1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
